Risk Management and Internal Control                                             風險管理及內部監控
The Board is responsible for maintaining appropriate and effective risk          董事會負責維護適當有效之風險管理及內部
management and internal control systems.to safeguard shareholders’               監控制度 ，以保障股東的投資 ，更有效控制
investments and have a better control on the business and operation of           本公司之業務及營運 。董事會亦負責每年檢
the Company. The Board is also responsible for reviewing the effectiveness       討風險管理及內部監控制度之成效 。
of its risk management and internal control systems on an annual basis.


The Company has maintained a tailored governance structure with defined          本公司設有切合本身所需之管治架構 ，明確
lines of responsibility and appropriate delegation of responsibility and         劃分職責 ，並向高級管理層轉授適當責任及
authority to the senior management. As a leading and comprehensive ICT           授權 。作為領先而全面的資訊通訊科技產品
products distributor, our Group has many well-known suppliers and vendors        分銷商 ，本集團有許多知名供應商及承辦商
as well as extensive downstream channel partners. In order to cooperate          以及大量下游渠道夥伴。為與各方充分合
with different parties, our senior management team will be delegated to          作 ，我們的高級管理層團隊將獲轉授不同責
take different responsibility such as product management, supply chain           任 ，各自負責產品管理 、供應鏈管理 、營運
management, operational management and financial management, etc., to            管理及財務管理等範疇 ，確保本集團業務運
ensure that the Group has a smooth business operation to reduce risk and         作暢順 ，減低可能出現之風險及不確定因素 。
uncertainties that may be caused.


The management of the Company is responsible for designing, implementing         本公司之管理層負責設計 、執行及監察本集
and monitoring the Group’s risk management and internal control framework,       團之風險管理及內部監控架構 ，範圍涵蓋所
covering all material controls including financial, operational and compliance   有重大監控 ，包括財務 、營運及合規監控 。
controls. The risk management and internal control framework also provides       風險管理及內部監控架構亦可識別及管理風
for identification and management of risk.                                       險。


The management also conducts periodic meetings and independent reviews           管理層亦針對個別部門之運作舉行定期會議
on the operations of individual divisions to identify any irregularities and     並進行獨立檢討 ，以識別有否任何違規事項
risks, develops action plans and recommendations to address the identified       及風險 、制定行動計劃 、提供推薦建議處理
risks, and reports to the Audit Committee on any key findings. The Audit         所識別之風險 ，並向審核委員會匯報任何重
Committee, in turn, reports to the Board on any material issues and makes        大發現 。審核委員會則向董事會匯報任何重
recommendations to the Board.                                                    大事項並提供推薦建議 。


The Board acknowledged that it is their responsibilities to maintain an          董事會確認其有責任維護有效之風險管理及
effective risk management and internal control systems. Such systems             內部監控制度 。該等制度旨在管理而非消除
are designed to manage rather than eliminate the risk of failure to achieve      未能實現業務目標之風險 ，且僅可就防止重
business objectives, and can only provide reasonable and not absolute            大錯誤陳述或損失提供合理而非絕對之保證 。
assurance against material misstatement or loss.

for identifying and considering the disclosure requirements and guidelines       有關內幕消息之披露規定及指引 。
regarding inside information.

Dividend Policy                                                                    股息政策
The dividend policy adopted by the Company is based on a number of                 本公司採用之股息政策乃基於多項因素及條

Key audit matters
Key audit matters are those matters that, in our professional judgement, were of most significance in our audit of the consolidated
financial statements of the current period. These matters were addressed in the context of our audit of the consolidated financial
statements as a whole, and in forming our opinion thereon, and we do not provide a separate opinion on these matters.


關鍵審計事項
關鍵審計事項是根據我們的專業判斷 ，認為對本期綜合財務報表的審計最為重要的事項 。這些事項是在我們審計整體
綜合財務報表及出具意見時進行處理的 。我們不會對這些事項提供單獨的意見 。


Valuation of inventories

   2.6 Goodwill                                                                   2.6 商譽
        Goodwill represents the excess of:                                              商譽指以下兩者之間的差額：


        (i)      the aggregate of the fair value of the consideration                   (i)    所轉讓代價的公平值、被
                 transferred, the amount of any non-controlling interest in                    收購方任何非控股權益的金
                 the acquiree and the fair value of the Group’s previously                     額及本集團此前於被收購方
                 held equity interest in the acquiree; over                                    所持股本權益的公平值之總
                                                                                               和；超過


        (ii)     the net fair value of the acquiree’s identifiable assets and           (ii)   被收購方於收購日期所計量
                 liabilities measured as at the acquisition date.                              可識別資產及負債的淨公平
                                                                                               值。


        When (ii) is greater than (i), then this excess is recognised                   當 (ii) 大 於 (i) 時 ， 此 差 額 立 即 於 損
        immediately in profit or loss as a gain on a bargain purchase.                  益賬中確認為議價收購收益 。


        Goodwill is stated at cost less accumulated impairment losses.                  商譽按成本扣除累計減值虧損列
        Goodwill arising on a business combination is allocated to each                 賬 。業務合併所產生之商譽分配
        cash generating unit, or groups of cash generating units, that is               至預期可從合併的協同效應中受
        expected to benefit from the synergies of the combination and                   惠之各個或各組現金產生單位，
        is tested annually for impairment (see note 2.7).                               並每年作減值測試（見附註2.7）。


        On disposal of a cash generating unit during the year, any                      於年內出售現金產生單位時，已
        attributable amount of purchased goodwill is included in the                    購入商譽應佔之任何金額乃於計
        calculation of the gain or loss on disposal.                                    算出售盈虧時納入其中 。

      2.16 Income tax                                                                   2.16 所得稅
            Income tax for the year comprises current tax and movements in                 年內所得稅包括本期稅項及遞延
            deferred tax assets and liabilities. Current tax and movements in              稅項資產和負債之變動 。本期稅
            deferred tax assets and liabilities are recognised in profit or loss           項及遞延稅項資產和負債之變動
            except to the extent that they relate to items recognised in other             均於損益賬內確認，惟與於其他
            comprehensive income or directly in equity, in which case the                  全面收入或直接於權益確認之項
            relevant amounts of tax are recognised in other comprehensive                  目相關者除外，在此情況下，有
            income or directly in equity, respectively.                                    關稅項金額亦分別於其他全面收
                                                                                           入或直接於權益內確認 。


            Current tax is calculated on the basis of the tax laws enacted                 本期稅項按於報告期末本公司及
            or substantively enacted at the end of the reporting period                    其附屬公司、聯營公司和合營企
            in the countries where the Company and its subsidiaries,                       業經營及產生應課稅收入所在國
            associates and joint ventures operate and generate taxable                     家已制訂或實質上已制訂之稅法
            income. Management periodically evaluates positions taken in                   計算 。管理層就適用稅務規例須
            tax returns with respect to situations in which applicable tax                 作出詮釋之情況定期評估在報稅
            regulation is subject to interpretation and establishes provisions             表上填報之狀況，並按預期將向
            where appropriate on the basis of amounts expected to be paid                  稅務部門繳付之金額確立適當撥
            to the tax authorities.                                                        備。


            Deferred tax is provided in full, using the liability method,                  遞延稅項採用負債法就資產及負
            on temporary differences arising between the tax bases of                      債之稅基與其在財務報表中之賬
            assets and liabilities and their carrying amounts in the financial             面金額兩者間之暫時差異作全數
            statements. However, deferred tax is not accounted for if it arises            撥備 。然而，倘遞延稅項來自首
            from initial recognition of an asset or liability in a transaction other       次 確 認 一 項 交 易（ 並 非 業 務 合 併 ，
            than a business combination that at the time of the transaction                且於交易時並不影響會計或應課
            affects neither accounting nor taxable profit or loss.                         稅 損 益 ）中 之 資 產 或 負 債 ， 則 該 遞
                                                                                           延稅項不予入賬 。


            Deferred tax is determined using tax rates and laws that have                  遞延稅項以於報告期末已制訂或
            been enacted or substantively enacted at the end of the reporting              實質上已制訂及預期於相關遞延
            period and are expected to apply when the related deferred tax                 稅項資產變現或遞延稅項負債清
            assets are realised or the deferred tax liabilities are settled.               償時適用之稅率及稅法釐定 。遞
            Deferred tax assets and liabilities are not discounted.                        延稅項資產及負債不作貼現 。

   2.18 Provisions and contingent liabilities                                     2. 18 撥備及或然負債
        Provisions are recognised for liabilities of uncertain timing                   當本集團或本公司因過往事件而
        or amount when the Group or the Company has a legal or                          出現法律或推定責任，且履行有
        constructive obligation arising as a result of a past event, it is              關責任可能需要經濟利益流出，
        probable that an outflow of economic benefits will be required                  並可對此作出可靠的估計時，即
        to settle the obligation and a reliable estimate can be made.                   會對時間或金額不確定之負債確
        Where the time value of money is material, provisions are stated                認撥備。倘貨幣時間價值屬重
        at the present value of the expenditure expected to settle the                  大，則撥備按預期履行責任所需
        obligation.                                                                     開支之現值列賬 。

        Where it is not probable that an outflow of economic benefits                   倘不大可能需要經濟利益流出，
        will be required, or the amount cannot be estimated reliably,                   或無法可靠估計有關金額，則該
        the obligation is disclosed as a contingent liability, unless the               責任乃披露為或然負債，除非經
        probability of outflow of economic benefits is remote. Possible                 濟利益流出之可能性極微 。可能
        obligations, whose existence will only be confirmed by the                      承 擔 之 責 任（ 其 存 在 與 否 將 僅 由 是
        occurrence or non-occurrence of one or more future events are                   否發生一項或多項未來事件確認）
        also disclosed as contingent liabilities unless the probability of              亦披露為或然負債，除非經濟利
        outflow of economic benefits is remote.                                         益流出之可能性極微 。

      2.19 Leases                                                                     2.19 租賃
            An arrangement, comprising a transaction or a series of                      倘本集團認為某項安排轉讓一項
            transactions, is or contains a lease if the Group determines                 權利，可在協定時期內使用一項
            that the arrangement conveys a right to use a specific asset or              或多項特定資產以換取一筆或一
            assets for an agreed period of time in return for a payment or a             連 串 款 項 ， 則 該 安 排（ 包 括 一 項
            series of payments. Such a determination is made based on an                 或 一 連 串 交 易 ）屬 於 或 包 括 一 項 租
            evaluation of the substance of the arrangement and is regardless             賃 。有關決定乃根據對安排之實
            of whether the arrangement takes the legal form of a lease.                  質內容之評估作出，而不論該安
                                                                                         排是否具備租賃之法定形式 。


            (a)     Operating leases                                                     (a)   經營租賃
                    Leases in which a significant portion of the risks and                     擁有權之大部分風險及回報
                    rewards of ownership are retained by the lessor are                        由出租人保留之租賃分類為
                    classified as operating leases. Payments made under                        經營租賃 。根據經營租賃支
                    operating leases (net of any incentives received from the                  付之款項（扣除自出租人取得
                    lessor) are charged to profit or loss on a straight-line basis             之 任 何 優 惠 後 ）在 租 期 內 以
                    over the period of the lease. Lease incentives received                    直線法自損益賬扣除 。所取
                    are recognised in profit or loss as an integral part of the                得之租賃優惠於損益賬內確
                    aggregate net lease payments made. Contingent rentals                      認為淨租金付款總額之一部
                    are charged to profit or loss in the accounting period in                  分 。或然租金在其產生之會
                    which they are incurred.                                                   計期間自損益賬扣除 。


            (b)     Finance leases                                                       (b)   融資租賃
                    The Group leases certain property, plant and equipment.                    本集團租用若干物業 、廠房
                    Leases of property, plant and equipment where the Group                    及設備。本集團擁有絕大
                    has substantially all the risks and rewards of ownership are               部分擁有權風險及回報之物
                    classified as finance leases. Finance leases are capitalised               業 、廠房及設備之租賃分類
                    at the lease’s commencement at the lower of the fair                       為融資租賃 。融資租賃於租

                    minimum lease payments.                                                    及最低租賃付款現值之間的

            excluding those amounts collected on behalf of third parties.           表第三方收取的款項 。收益不包
            Revenue excludes value added tax or other sales taxes and is            括增值稅或其他銷售稅，並已扣
            after deduction of any trade discounts.                                 除任何貿易折扣 。


            Where the contract contains a financing component which                 倘合約載有向客戶提供重大融資
            provides a significant financing benefit to the customer for more       福 利 超 過 12 個 月 的 融 資 組 成 部
            than 12 months, revenue is measured at the present value of             分，收益按應收款項之現值計
            the amount receivable, discounted using the discount rate that          量，採用將於與客戶單獨融資交
            would be reflected in a separate financing transaction with the         易中反映的貼現率進行貼現，而
            customer, and interest income is accrued separately under               利息收入則按實際利息法單獨應
            the effective interest method. Where the contract contains a            計 。倘合約載有向本集團提供重
            financing component which provides a significant financing              大融資福利的融資組成部分，則
            benefit to the Group, revenue recognized under that contract            根據該合約確認收益包括採用實
            includes the interest expense accreted on the contract liability        際利息法就合約負債應計的利息
            under the effective interest method. The Group takes advantage          開支 。本集團利用香港財務報告
            of the practical expedient in paragraph 63 of HKFRS 15 and              準 則 第 15 號 第 63 段 的 實 際 權 宜 方
            does not adjust the consideration for any effects of a significant      法 ， 並 不 調 整 在 融 資 期 間 為 12 個

3. Financial Risk Management and Fair Values                                       3. 財務風險管理及公平值
      3.1 Financial risk factors                                                    3.1 財務風險因素
            The Group’s activities expose it to a variety of financial risks:         本集團之業務承受外匯風險、利
            foreign exchange risk, interest rate risk, credit risk and liquidity      率風險、信貸風險及流動性風險
            risk. The Group’s overall risk management program focuses on              等多項財務風險 。本集團的整體
            the unpredictability of financial markets and seeks to minimise           風險管理計劃着重金融市場之不
            potential adverse effects on the Group’s financial performance.           可預測性，並尋求盡量減輕對本
                                                                                      集團財務表現之潛在不利影響 。


            (a)   Foreign exchange risk                                               (a)   外匯風險
                  The Group operates in various countries and is exposed                    本集團在多個國家經營業
                  to foreign exchange risk arising from various currency                    務 ，承受來自不同貨幣之外
                  exposures, primarily with respect to Chinese Renminbi                     匯風險 ，風險主要涉及人民
                  (“RMB”), the United States Dollar (“US$”), the Singapore                  幣、美元、新加坡元、泰
                  Dollar (“S$”), Thai Baht (“THB”) and Indonesian Rupiah                    銖及印尼盾 。外匯風險來自
                  (“RP”). Foreign exchange risk arises from future commercial               海外業務之未來商業交易 、

      3.2 Capital risk management                                                3.2 資本風險管理
            The Group’s objectives when managing capital are to safeguard              本集團管理資本之目的為確保本
            the Group’s ability to continue as a going concern in order                集團有能力持續營運，以為股東
            to provide returns for shareholders and benefits for other                 提供回報及為其他持份者提供利
            stakeholders and to maintain an optimal capital structure to               益，以及維持最佳資本架構以減
            reduce the cost of capital.                                                低資本成本 。

            In order to maintain or adjust the capital structure, the Group            為維持或調整資本架構，本集
            may adjust the amount of dividends paid to shareholders, return            團或會調整派付予股東之股息金
            capital to shareholders, issue new shares or sell assets to                額、向股東發還資本、發行新股
            reduce debt.                                                               份或售資減債 。

            The Group monitors capital on the basis of the net debt/                   本集團按債務淨額╱總資產比率
            total assets ratio. This ratio is calculated as net debt divided           監察資本 。該比率按債務淨額除
            by total assets. Net debt is calculated as total borrowings                以總資產計算 。債務淨額按總借
            (including current and non-current borrowings as shown in                  貸（ 包 括 綜 合 財 務 狀 況 表 所 示 之 流
            the consolidated statement of financial position) less cash and            動 及 非 流 動 借 貸 ）減 現 金 及 現 金 等
            cash equivalents. Total asset is calculated as shown in the                價物計算 。總資產按綜合財務狀
            consolidated statement of financial position.                              況表所示計算 。

            The table below analyses the Group’s capital structure as at               下表分析本集團於二零一八年十
            31 December 2018:                                                          二月三十一日之資本架構：

                    The fair value of forward contracts in Level 2 is                              第二層級遠期合約之公平值

                    price and deducting the current spot rate. The discount                        去當前現貨價格釐定 。所使
                    rate used is derived from the relevant government yield                        用之貼現率按報告期末之有
                    curve as at the end of the reporting period plus an                            關政府收益率曲線 ，另加足
                    adequate constant credit spread.                                               夠固定信貸息差得出 。

                    The fair value of securities in Level 2 is determined                          第二層級證券之公平值乃按
                    by the Black-Scholes model. The volatility used is                             柏力舒爾斯模型釐定 。所使
                    derived from the historical quote price of the underlying                      用之波幅按相關證券之以往
                    securities.                                                                    報價得出 。

            not be realised.                                                                  存貨撇減記賬 。

      reference to sales generated by those segments and the expenses               額及該等分部產生之開支分配至該等分
      incurred by those segments.                                                   部。

      within the segment.                                                           行政費用 。

23. Taxation (continued)                                                         23. 稅項（續）
      Taxation outside Hong Kong has been calculated based on the                  香港以外稅項按年內估計應課稅溢利以
      estimated assessable profit for the year at rates of taxation prevailing     本集團旗下實體經營所在管轄權當時之
      in the jurisdictions in which the entities comprising the Group operate.     稅率計算 。
